-> Strategy brain, version 2:
Fixed-limit hold'em, 4 players, many multiway pots. Tight-aggressive, value-focused.
Preflop: open/raise strong hands (pairs, AK, AQ, KQs, suited broadways, big suited connectors) mainly in late position. Fold weak offsuit junk (e.g. 4-2, 5-4 offsuit) even cheaply from blinds. Limp pairs and broadways in position are fine; raise with pairs for value.
Postflop: bet strong made hands and good draws; with a set or better bet/raise for value. Check-call one-pair hands on multi-way boards; fold to heavy action without top pair or a real draw. Pot-control with medium hands when checked to.
Opponents: A02 and A03 call very loosely and often reach the river; value-bet them relentlessly, don't bluff them. A02 raised flops with medium hands. A03 bets rivers after checking, sometimes with air; call a single river bet with a pair. A01 raises preflop and bets scare-card turns; respect its raises.
Keep notes short; update each game.

Fixed-limit hold'em, 4 players, many multiway pots. Tight-aggressive, value-focused.

Postflop: bet top pair, two pair, sets and good draws for value; bet checked turns/rivers (opponents check through and call). Fold to a flop bet with only backdoor draws. Check-call one-pair hands on multiway boards.
